与智能光标（Ctrl 选目标格，已移植 src/player/SmartCursor.ts）是**独立系统**：Smart Select = **Shift 单键自动换手持工具**（PlayerInput.cs:1898/2011/2123）。
- `Player.SmartSelectLookup`（Player.cs:17283-17309）：以鼠标格 tX/tY → `SmartSelect_GetToolStrategy`（:17472-17530）按优先级选策略：7=提取器（219/642）、6=大炮（209）、1=锤、2=斧、8=刮漆器、3=镐、4=水桶（湿格且自身 wet）、5=超 200px 距离修正；然后 `SmartSelect_PickToolForStrategy`（:17321 起）在物品栏 0-49 格找对应工具（火把/荧光棒、镐、斧、锤、桶…）自动切过去（selectedItem 切换 + 粘滞 _lastSmartCursorToolStrategy）。
- 与智能光标共存（可同按）。

- 每帧（updateUse 头部，智能光标 lookup 附近）：Shift 按住 → 按原版策略优先级对鼠标格选工具 → 找到就切 selectedItem（粘滞语义 1:1）。
- 工具匹配谓词复用：镐/斧/锤 = tool.type；提取器/大炮 = tile 判定；桶 = useBucket 链的水桶 key；刮漆器若无油漆系统按原版策略号跳过（注释行号+"等价=无该物品"）。
- 测试 tests/smart-select.test.ts（≥6 例：Shift 指向镐可挖块自动换镐/指向树换斧/指向锤族换锤/指向提取器换提取器/超 200px 修正策略 5/粘滞语义/无匹配不切）；tsc 零错误+相关测试全绿；全量报告（他人 WIP 失败隔离确认）。

using System;
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;

namespace Terraria.GameInput;

public class SmartSelectGamepadPointer
{
	private Vector2 _size;

	private Vector2 _center;

	private Vector2 _distUniform = new Vector2(80f, 64f);

	public bool ShouldBeUsed()
	{
		if (PlayerInput.UsingGamepad && Main.LocalPlayer.controlTorch)
		{
			return Main.SmartCursorIsUsed;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public void SmartSelectLookup_GetTargetTile(Player player, out int tX, out int tY)
	{
		tX = (int)(((float)Main.mouseX + Main.screenPosition.X) / 16f);
		tY = (int)(((float)Main.mouseY + Main.screenPosition.Y) / 16f);
		if (player.gravDir == -1f)
		{
			tY = (int)((Main.screenPosition.Y + (float)Main.screenHeight - (float)Main.mouseY) / 16f);
		}
		if (ShouldBeUsed())
		{
			Point point = GetPointerPosition().ToPoint();
			tX = (int)(((float)point.X + Main.screenPosition.X) / 16f);
			tY = (int)(((float)point.Y + Main.screenPosition.Y) / 16f);
			if (player.gravDir == -1f)
			{
				tY = (int)((Main.screenPosition.Y + (float)Main.screenHeight - (float)point.Y) / 16f);
			}
		}
	}

	public void UpdateSize(Vector2 size)
	{
		_size = size;
	}

	public void UpdateCenter(Vector2 center)
	{
		_center = center;
	}

	public Vector2 GetPointerPosition()
	{
		Vector2 vector = (new Vector2(Main.mouseX, Main.mouseY) - _center) / _size;
		float num = Math.Abs(vector.X);
		if (num < Math.Abs(vector.Y))
		{
			num = Math.Abs(vector.Y);
		}
		if (num > 1f)
		{
			vector /= num;
		}
		vector *= Main.GameViewMatrix.RenderZoom.X;
		return vector * _distUniform + _center;
	}
}

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Key understanding: `PickItemSelectionOverride` → `SmartSelectLookup` is called every frame from `SelectedItem.update()` when `CanChangeSelectedItemImmediately`. The "sticky" semantics: when Shift released (controlTorch false), `SmartSelectLookup` returns false with `_lastSmartCursorToolStrategy = -1` reset, so the override is cleared and selection returns to... `HasActiveOverride` — `OverrideSelection` sets `overridden`, and when shift released, overridden cleared, selected returns to `num`... Actually `selected` stays as the override since it was assigned; on release, `HasBufferedChange` false and `HasActiveOverride` false → selected stays. Hmm, actually in vanilla when you release Shift, your item returns to the originally held item? No — vanilla: while holding Shift, it shows the smart-selected item; when released, it returns to the previously selected item (the "override" is temporary). Let me check `OverrideSelection` and related fields.
```

	public struct SelectedItemState(Player player)
	{
		private readonly Player player = player;

		private int selected = 0;

		private int hotbar = 0;

		private int buffered = -1;

		private int overridden = -1;

		public bool CanChangeSelectedItemImmediately
		{
			get
			{
				if (!player.UsingOrReusingItem)
				{
					return player.ItemTimeIsZero;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public int Selected => selected;

		public int Hotbar => hotbar;

		public bool HasActiveOverride => overridden >= 0;

		public bool HasBufferedChange => buffered >= 0;

		public int LastNonOverridenSelection
		{
			get
			{
				if (!HasBufferedChange)
				{
					if (HasActiveOverride)
					{
						return -1;
					}
					return selected;
				}
				return buffered;
			}
		}

		private void OverrideSelection(int item)
		{
			if (CanChangeSelectedItemImmediately && item != selected)
			{
				if (overridden == -1)
				{
					overridden = selected;
				}
				if (item == overridden)
				{
					overridden = -1;
				}
				selected = item;

```
Now I understand vanilla sticky semantics:
- `OverrideSelection(item)`: if item != selected: if overridden == -1, overridden = selected (remember original); if item == overridden, overridden = -1 (restoring to original clears override marker); selected = item.
- `Update()`: each frame — if buffered, selected = buffered; else if HasActiveOverride, selected = overridden (restore original); then cleared buffered/overridden; then PickItemSelectionOverride → if smart select active, OverrideSelection(slot).

So while Shift is held: every frame it restores `selected = overridden` (the original) and then overrides to the smart-picked slot. When Shift released: `HasActiveOverride` restores `selected = overridden` = the original item. That's the sticky/return semantics: hold shift → temp switch, release → revert to original.

Note: while holding Shift, each frame `selected` is set back to `overridden` (original) then override picks new slot — that means overridden stays at original (since `OverrideSelection` only sets overridden when overridden == -1). Wait — after restore, overridden is cleared to -1 (line `buffered = -1; overridden = -1;`) — so next frame OverrideSelection sets overridden = selected again. Fine.

Important detail: the "sticky" mention in task — `_lastSmartCursorToolStrategy` sticky for gamepad. For keyboard, `_lastSmartCursorToolStrategy = toolStrategy` is stored each frame but only used when gamepad. Also when controlTorch is false, it's reset to -1.

Now let me examine our codebase: SmartCursor.ts, Input.ts, Inventory, Game.ts updateUse.
```

</details>

// ===================== 手持物语义解析 =====================

/** 物品原版 id（def.vid 优先，vi_NNNN_ key 前缀兜底） */
export function vanillaItemIdOf(def: ItemDef | null | undefined): number | null {
  if (!def) return null;
  if (def.vid != null) return def.vid;
  const m = /^vi_(\d+)_/.exec(def.key);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : null;
}

interface HeldSemantics {
  vid: number | null;
  pick: number; axe: number; hammer: number;
  createTileSheet: number | null;
  createWall: number | null;
  placeStyle: number;
}

function heldSemantics(held: SmartCursorHeld): HeldSemantics {
  const def = held.def;
  const tool = held.tool;
  return {
    vid: vanillaItemIdOf(def),
    pick: tool?.type === 'pick' ? tool.power : 0,
    // 副斧力（Drax 579 等镐+斧双工具 / Hamaxe 锤+斧）：主工具之外再取 def.axePower
    axe: tool?.type === 'axe' ? tool.power : (def?.axePower ?? 0),
    hammer: tool?.type === 'hammer' ? tool.power : 0,
    createTileSheet: def?.tile != null ? sheetOf(TILE_BY_KEY[def.tile] ?? -1) : null,
    createWall: def?.wallId != null ? def.wallId : null,
    placeStyle: def?.placeStyle ?? 0,
  };
}

1	// 键盘/鼠标输入。UI 打开时屏蔽游戏输入。
2	export class Input {
3	  keys = new Set<string>();
4	  mouseX = 0; mouseY = 0;
5	  mouseDown = false;
6	  rightDown = false;
7	  uiBlocking = false;     // 背包/合成等打开时为 true
8	  wheelDelta = 0;
9	  keydownHandlers: Array<(code: string) => void> = [];
10	
11	  private el: HTMLElement;
12	  /** 已注册监听(destroy 时全部移除——window 强引用曾把整个旧 Game 钉死,
13	   *  连续多次读档 GPU 内存累积 → contextlost 风暴卡死,2026-08-10 trace 实证) */
14	  private winListeners: Array<[string, EventListener, AddEventListenerOptions?]> = [];
15	  private elListeners: Array<[string, EventListener, AddEventListenerOptions?]> = [];
16	
17	  constructor(el: HTMLElement) {
18	    this.el = el;
19	    const onWin = (type: string, fn: EventListener, opts?: AddEventListenerOptions) => {
20	      window.addEventListener(type, fn, opts);
21	      this.winListeners.push([type, fn, opts]);
22	    };
23	    const onEl = (type: string, fn: EventListener, opts?: AddEventListenerOptions) => {
24	      el.addEventListener(type, fn, opts);
25	      this.elListeners.push([type, fn, opts]);
26	    };
27	
28	    onWin('keydown', ((e: KeyboardEvent) => {
29	      if (e.repeat) return;
30	      this.keys.add(e.code);
31	      this.keydownHandlers.forEach((fn) => fn(e.code));
32	      if (['Space', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'Tab'].includes(e.code)) e.preventDefault();
33	    }) as EventListener);
34	    onWin('keyup', ((e: KeyboardEvent) => { this.keys.delete(e.code); }) as EventListener);
35	    onWin('blur', (() => this.keys.clear()) as EventListener);
36	    onEl('mousemove', ((e: MouseEvent) => {
37	      const r = this.el.getBoundingClientRect();

39	      this.mouseY = e.clientY - r.top;
40	    }) as EventListener);

42	    onWin('mousedown', ((e: MouseEvent) => {
43	      // UI 面板上的点击不穿透为游戏输入
44	      if ((e.target as HTMLElement)?.closest?.('.sw-panel, .sw-slot, .sw-recipe, .sw-btn')) return;
45	      if (e.button === 0) this.mouseDown = true;
46	      if (e.button === 2) this.rightDown = true;
47	    }) as EventListener);

49	      if (e.button === 0) this.mouseDown = false;
50	      if (e.button === 2) this.rightDown = false;
51	    }) as EventListener);
52	    onEl('contextmenu', (e) => e.preventDefault());

54	      this.wheelDelta += Math.sign(e.deltaY);
55	      e.preventDefault();
56	    }) as EventListener, { passive: false });
57	  }

Note: keydown with `if (e.repeat) return;` — that's a bug for holding Shift actually no, keys.add happens on first keydown, keys stays until keyup. Repeat just skips re-adding. Fine.

  /** 智能光标设置（对齐原版 Player.SmartCursorSettings + Main.cs:49705 设置结构） */
  private smartSettings(): SmartCursorSettings {
    const d = options.data;
    return {
      toggleMode: d.smartCursorMode === 'toggle',
      smartAxeAfterPickaxe: !!d.smartAxeAfterPickaxe,
      smartBlocksEnabled: !!d.smartBlocksEnabled,
      holdCanReleaseMidUse: !!d.smartHoldCanReleaseMidUse,
    };
  }

  /**
   * 智能光标每帧驱动（Player.cs:25159-25163 每帧 SmartCursorLookup + :28344-28374 Toggle/Hold 状态机）。
   * Ctrl 按下沿翻转（Toggle）/跟随（Hold）；切换播音效 12（menuTick）。
   * 必须在 updateUse 之前跑——同帧 lookup 结果即被挖掘/放置路径覆盖 tx/ty。
   */
  private updateSmartCursor() {
    const inp = this.input;
    const sc = this.smartCursor;
    const s = this.smartSettings();
    const controlSmart = inp.down('ControlLeft') || inp.down('ControlRight');
    if (sc.updateWanted(controlSmart, !!inp.mouseDown, s)) this.sfx.play('menuTick');
    const blocked = inp.uiBlocking || this.player.dead || !!this.minecart
      || this.renderer.fullMap.open || this.renderer.timeUiHover || this.renderer.weatherUiHover
      || this.annotateMode;
    if (blocked || !sc.wanted) { sc.showing = false; VUI.smartCursorOn = false; return; }
    const held = this.player.inv.heldItem();
    const heldDef = held ? ITEM_DEFS[held.id] : null;
    const tool = heldDef?.tool ?? (held ? this.itemFuncTool(held.id) : undefined);
    const [mx, my] = this.camera.screenToWorld(inp.mouseX, inp.mouseY);
    const p = this.player;
    // 放置物才有 blockRange（原版 SmartCursorLookup :95-98 createWall/createTile 才加）；
    // 工具 tileBoost=0
    const placing = heldDef?.tile != null || heldDef?.wallId != null;
    const tb = placing
      ? (p.equipStats.tileRange ? 3 : 0) + (p.buffs.has(BuffType.Builder) ? 1 : 0)
      : 0;
    // EmptyTile 的占位实体（玩家 + 敌怪 + 城镇 NPC）
    const occupants: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }> = [
      { x: p.x, y: p.y, w: p.w, h: p.h },
    ];
    for (const e of this.entities.enemies) occupants.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, w: e.w, h: e.h });
    for (const e of this.entities.npcs) occupants.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, w: e.w, h: e.h });
    sc.lookup({
      st: this.world.store,
      mouse: { x: mx, y: my },
      actor: { x: p.x, y: p.y, w: p.w, h: p.h, cx: p.cx, cy: p.cy, facing: p.facing },
      held: { def: heldDef, tool: tool ? { type: tool.type, power: tool.power ?? 0 } : null },
      settings: s,
      tileBoost: tb,
      wireToolMode: this.wireToolMode,
      occupants,
      controlUseItem: !!inp.mouseDown,
      // Step_AlchemySeeds 成熟药草判定（WorldGen.cs:66198-66225 IsAlchemyPlantHarvestable）
      // 所需世界态；Main.time 口径 = 白天 (t-0.25)*54000 / 夜晚 (t-0.75 / t+0.25)*32400
      alchemy: {
        dayTime: this.world.clock.isDay,
        bloodMoon: this.world.clock.bloodMoon,
        moonPhase: this.world.clock.moonPhase,
        raining: this.world.weather.raining,
        cloudAlpha: this.world.weather.cloudAlpha,
        time: vanillaMainTime(this.world.clock.timeOfDay),
        worldSurface: this.world.groundLevel,
        remixWorld: false,
        maxTilesY: this.world.store.h,
      },
    });
    // 指针切到 Cursors[12] 智能光标帧（Main.cs:47442-47513 num=11+smart.ToInt()）
    VUI.smartCursorOn = sc.showing;
  }

    // 直接用 canvas 的 CSS 矩形 + 相机中心/缩放换算,保证以指针为起点
    const rect = this.renderer.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

    const sy = (this.input.mouseY || rect.height / 2) - rect.height / 2;
    const z = this.camera.zoom;
    const wx = sx / z + this.camera.x;
    const wy = sy / z + this.camera.y;
    const st = this.world.store;
    // fits(tx, ty):ty = 脚下实心行,其上 3 行 2 列净空(树干等非实心不挡)
    const fits = (tx: number, ty: number): boolean => {
      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty) || !st.inBounds(tx + TELEPORT_FIT_W - 1, ty - TELEPORT_FIT_H)) return false;
      for (let dx = 0; dx < TELEPORT_FIT_W; dx++) {
        if (!st.isSolid(tx + dx, ty)) return false;
        for (let dy = 1; dy <= TELEPORT_FIT_H; dy++) {
          if (st.isActive(tx + dx, ty - dy)) return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    };
    const tx = Math.floor(wx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(wy / TILE);
    let land: [number, number] | null = fits(tx, ty) ? [tx, ty] : null;
    // 环形 BFS(Chebyshev 距离逐环外扩):第 r 环上的全部格按"上边→下边→左边→右边"
    // 顺序找,保证命中的是距鼠标最近的落脚点(同环内顺序只影响并列情形)
    outer: for (let r = 1; r <= TELEPORT_RING_MAX && !land; r++) {
      for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
        for (const dy of [-r, r]) {
          if (fits(tx + dx, ty + dy)) { land = [tx + dx, ty + dy]; break outer; }
        }
      }
      for (let dy = -r + 1; dy <= r - 1; dy++) {
        for (const dx of [-r, r]) {
          if (fits(tx + dx, ty + dy)) { land = [tx + dx, ty + dy]; break outer; }
        }
      }
    }
    if (!land) {
      this.cb.onToast?.('瞬移失败:附近没有可落脚点');
      return;
    }

  constructor(root: HTMLElement, cb: GameCallbacks, atlas?: SpriteAtlas | null) {
    this.assets = buildAssets();
    if (atlas) {
      this.atlas = atlas;
      this.autotiler = new AutoTiler(atlas);
    }
    this.renderer = new Renderer(this.assets, atlas);
    this.renderer.attach(root);
    // 音效按需加载（2026-08-10）：不在此预读——播放未命中时后台 fetch+decode、
    // 首播合成兜底；常用战斗/交互声在世界就绪后小集合预热（见 afterWorldLoad），
    // 怪物专属声效随首次受击懒加载
    this.input = new Input(this.renderer.canvas);
    this.cb = cb;
    // 快捷栏数字键：事件驱动（避免单帧内 down+up 被轮询漏掉）
    this.input.keydownHandlers.push((code) => {
      if (this.player && /^Digit[0-9]$/.test(code)) {
        this.player.inv.selected = code === 'Digit0' ? 9 : Number(code.slice(5)) - 1;
        this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
      }
      // 缩放调节
      if (code === 'Equal' || code === 'NumpadAdd') this.setZoom(this.camera?.zoomTarget + 0.15);
      if (code === 'Minus' || code === 'NumpadSubtract') this.setZoom(this.camera?.zoomTarget - 0.15);

  private itemFuncTool(id: number): (typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number]['tool'] {
    const def = ITEM_DEFS[id];
    if (!def) return undefined;
    const m = /^vi_(\d+)_/.exec(def.key);
    if (!m) return undefined;
    const f = itemFuncOfVid(+m[1]);
    if (!f || !(f.pick || f.axe || f.hammer)) return undefined;
    const c = itemCombat(+m[1]);
    return {
      type: f.pick ? 'pick' : f.axe ? 'axe' : 'hammer',
      power: f.pick ?? f.axe ?? f.hammer,
      damage: c?.damage ?? 5,
      speed: c?.useAnimation ?? 25,
      useTime: c?.useTime ?? 20,
      knockback: c?.knockBack ?? 3,
      // 双工具副力（单工具时与主 power 同值，dualToolResolve 只在多力时改道）
      pickPower: f.pick ?? undefined,
      axePower: f.axe ?? undefined,
      hammerPower: f.hammer ?? undefined,
    } as NonNullable<(typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number]['tool']>;
  }

  /** 通用消耗品语义门（vanilla-itemfunc）：必须确有 heal/buff 语义才允许占用
   *  else-if 槽位——生命水晶(29)/魔力水晶(109)/生命果(1291)/弓枪族等无此语义，
   *  若被本分支空认领会把链条后续分支全部饿死（else-if 匹配即终止） */
  private consumableFuncGate(def: (typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number]): boolean {
    const fm = /^vi_(\d+)_/.exec(def.key);
    const ff = fm ? itemFuncOfVid(+fm[1]) : null;
    return !!ff?.consumable && !ff.createTile && !ff.shoot && !ff.pick && !ff.axe && !ff.hammer

/** Main.rand.Next 语义封装（next(n)=0..n-1；nextRange(a,b)=a..b-1） */
export interface ExtractinatorRand {
  next(maxExclusive: number): number;
  nextRange(minInclusive: number, maxExclusive: number): number;
}

export function mathRand(): ExtractinatorRand {
  return {
    next: (n) => Math.floor(Math.random() * n),
    nextRange: (a, b) => a + Math.floor(Math.random() * (b - a)),
  };
}

 */

	private void PlaceThing_ItemInExtractinator(ref ItemCheckContext context)
	{
		Tile tile = Main.tile[tileTargetX, tileTargetY];
		Item item = inventory[selectedItem];
		if (!tile.active() || !IsInTileInteractionRange(tileTargetX, tileTargetY, TileReachCheckSettings.Simple, item.tileBoost + blockRange) || !ItemTimeIsZero || itemAnimation <= 0 || !controlUseItem)

	public bool TryGetTradeOption(Item item, out TradeOption option)
	{
		option = null;
		int type = item.type;
		int stack = item.stack;
		for (int i = 0; i < _options.Count; i++)
		{
			TradeOption tradeOption = _options[i];
			if (tradeOption.WillTradeFor(type, stack))
			{
				option = tradeOption;
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static ItemTrader CreateChlorophyteExtractinator()
	{
		ItemTrader itemTrader = new ItemTrader();
		itemTrader.AddOption_Interchangable(12, 699);
		itemTrader.AddOption_Interchangable(11, 700);
		itemTrader.AddOption_Interchangable(14, 701);

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Terraria.GameContent;

public class ItemTrader
{
	public class TradeOption
	{
		public int TakingItemType;

		public int TakingItemStack;

		public int GivingItemType;

		public int GivingItemStack;

		public bool WillTradeFor(int offeredItemType, int offeredItemStack)
		{
			if (offeredItemType != TakingItemType || offeredItemStack < TakingItemStack)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}

	public static ItemTrader ChlorophyteExtractinator = CreateChlorophyteExtractinator();

	private List<TradeOption> _options = new List<TradeOption>();

	public void AddOption_Interchangable(int itemType1, int itemType2)
	{
		AddOption_OneWay(itemType1, 1, itemType2, 1);
		AddOption_OneWay(itemType2, 1, itemType1, 1);
	}

	public void AddOption_CyclicLoop(params int[] typesInOrder)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < typesInOrder.Length - 1; i++)
		{
			AddOption_OneWay(typesInOrder[i], 1, typesInOrder[i + 1], 1);
		}
		AddOption_OneWay(typesInOrder[^1], 1, typesInOrder[0], 1);
	}

	public void AddOption_FromAny(int givingItemType, params int[] takingItemTypes)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < takingItemTypes.Length; i++)
		{
			AddOption_OneWay(takingItemTypes[i], 1, givingItemType, 1);
		}
	}

	public void AddOption_OneWay(int takingItemType, int takingItemStack, int givingItemType, int givingItemStack)
	{
		_options.Add(new TradeOption
		{
			TakingItemType = takingItemType,
			TakingItemStack = takingItemStack,
			GivingItemType = givingItemType,

```

// 内部 tile / wall 注册表。id 即数组下标。
// 数值（硬度/光照）为原创平衡设计；mapColor 参考 TEdit tiles.json 的地图配色后重新取整。

export interface TileDef {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  solid: boolean;        // 参与碰撞
  platform: boolean;     // 单向平台（下跳可穿）
  decor: boolean;        // 装饰物（不碰撞、可被任何工具秒清）
  framed: boolean;       // 多格框架物体（使用 frameX/frameY 定位贴图）
  opacity: number;       // 光照阻挡 0-1
  light?: [number, number, number]; // 自发光 RGB
  pick: number;          // 所需镐力（-1 不可挖）
  axe: number;           // 所需斧力
  mapColor: string;      // 小地图颜色
  drop?: string | null;   // 破坏掉落 item key（缺省掉同名 key；null = 显式无掉落,原版装饰类）
  rope?: boolean;        // 绳索类（攀爬判定）
  blend: boolean;        // 是否参与同类边缘融合
  attach?: 'ground' | 'wall'; // 装饰物附着需求
  w?: number; h?: number;     // framed 物体占格数
  vanilla?: {                 // 原版素材渲染（terraria-assets + TEdit 数据）
    sheet: number;            // Tiles_N 表 id
    frame: 'auto' | 'style' | 'blend'; // auto=47 表 8 向；style=显式帧；blend=BlendRules 合并分帧（泥土/石/草族）
    fw?: number; fh?: number; // style 类的占格数（TEdit frameSize）
  };
}

export const T = {
  EMPTY: 0, DIRT: 1, STONE: 2, GRASS: 3,
  ORE_COPPER: 4, ORE_IRON: 5, ORE_SILVER: 6, ORE_GOLD: 7,
  TREE: 8, LEAVES: 9, WOOD: 10, PLATFORM: 11,
  TORCH: 12, WORKBENCH: 13, FURNACE: 14, ANVIL: 15,
  CHEST: 16, DOOR_CLOSED: 17, DOOR_OPEN: 18,
  MUSHROOM: 19, FLOWER: 20, TALLGRASS: 21, SAND: 22, SNOW: 23,
  SAPLING: 24, ASH: 25,
} as const;

export const TILE_DEFS: TileDef[] = [];
function def(d: Partial<TileDef> & { key: string }): number {
  const id = TILE_DEFS.length;
  TILE_DEFS.push({
    name: d.key, solid: false, platform: false, decor: false, framed: false,
    opacity: 0, pick: -1, axe: -1, mapColor: '#000', blend: false, ...d, id,
  } as TileDef);
  return id;
}
// 保证 id 与 T 常量一致（按顺序注册）
def({ key: 'empty', name: '空气' });
def({ key: 'dirt', name: '泥土', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#976B4B', blend: true, drop: 'dirt_block', vanilla: { sheet: 0, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'stone', name: '石块', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#808080', blend: true, drop: 'stone_block', vanilla: { sheet: 1, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'grass', name: '草块', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#1CD85E', blend: true, drop: 'dirt_block', vanilla: { sheet: 2, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'ore_copper', name: '铜矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#964316', blend: false, drop: 'copper_ore', vanilla: { sheet: 7, frame: 'auto' } });
def({ key: 'ore_iron', name: '铁矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#8C6550', blend: false, drop: 'iron_ore', vanilla: { sheet: 6, frame: 'auto' } });
def({ key: 'ore_silver', name: '银矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 20, mapColor: '#B9C2C3', blend: false, drop: 'silver_ore', vanilla: { sheet: 9, frame: 'auto' } });
def({ key: 'ore_gold', name: '金矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 40, mapColor: '#B9A417', blend: false, drop: 'gold_ore', vanilla: { sheet: 8, frame: 'auto' } });

const HAMMER_TILES = new Set([26, 31, 695, 696]);
function isHammerTile(sheet: number): boolean {
  return HAMMER_TILES.has(sheet);
}

/** Step_Platforms 辅助：格上是平台族 sheet（:1445-1452） */
function isPlatformSheet(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !activeAt(st, x, y)) return false;
  return PLATFORMS.has(sheetOf(st.get(x, y)));
}

// 道具功能画像运行时（tools/extract-itemfunc.mjs 从 1456 Item.cs 提取）。
// 用途：vi_ 批次物品的功能桥接——工具（镐/斧/锤挖掘）、药水（治疗/魔力/Buff）。
export interface ItemFunc {
  melee?: number; ranged?: number; magic?: number; summon?: number;
  noMelee?: number; damage?: number; mana?: number;
  pick?: number; axe?: number; hammer?: number;
  createTile?: number; createWall?: number; placeStyle?: number;
  consumable?: number; potion?: number;
  healLife?: number; healMana?: number;
  buffType?: number; buffTime?: number;
  ammo?: number; useAmmo?: number; notAmmo?: number;
  shoot?: number; channel?: number; holdStyle?: number; torch?: number;
  useStyle?: number; knockBack?: number; autoReuse?: number;
  fishingPole?: number;  // 钓竿力度（9 支；抛竿入口判定）
  bait?: number;        // 鱼饵力度（29 种；消耗率 1/(1+bait/6)）
}

import funcJson from './vanilla-itemfunc.json';

const FUNC = funcJson as Record<string, ItemFunc>;

/** 原版 item id → 功能画像 */
export function itemFuncOfVid(vid: number): ItemFunc | null {
  return FUNC[String(vid)] ?? null;
}

/** 内部 item id（vi_ key）→ 功能画像 */
export function itemFuncOfInternal(id: number, key: string): ItemFunc | null {
  const m = /^vi_(\d+)_/.exec(key);
  return m ? FUNC[m[1]] ?? null : null;
}

/** vi_ useAmmo 远程武器（弓/枪/发射器，DefaultToRangedWeapon 族 useStyle=5）：
 *  返回 useAmmo id（AmmoID.Arrow=40 / Bullet=97 …）；非 useAmmo 物品返回 0。
 *  combatWeapon 对 useAmmo 物品返回 null（弓弩/弹药体系另走 PickAmmo），Game 远程
 *  分支据此接管枪族——此前 vi_ 手枪/雪球炮等无 def.ranged 又被 combatWeapon 排除 = 静默失效 */
export function rangedAmmoWeapon(def: CombatWeaponItemLike): number {
  const vid = def.vid ?? viIdFromKey(def.key);
  if (vid < 0) return 0;
  const ua = itemCombat(vid)?.useAmmo ?? 0;
  return ua > 0 ? ua : 0;
}

// SoA 多通道平面数组存储。所有热点代码只碰 TypedArray。
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import { TileOpAction } from '../net/protocol';

export class TileStore {
  readonly w: number;
  readonly h: number;
  readonly type: Uint16Array;
  readonly flags: Uint8Array; // bit0 active bit1 framedDecor占位(预留)
  readonly frameX: Uint16Array;
  readonly frameY: Uint16Array;
  readonly wall: Uint16Array; // 原版 wld 墙 id（0-366）
  readonly liquid: Uint8Array;   // 0-255 液量
  readonly liquidType: Uint8Array; // 0无 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜 4微光(原版 liquidType 3,本仓库 +1 编码;任意液体遇微光 → Aetherium 块 659)
  readonly half: Uint8Array;      // halfBrick 位（原版 Tile.cs bTileHeader bit1）：下半 8px 实心
  readonly slope: Uint8Array;     // 坡面 0-4（原版编码；渲染暂未支持，生成期少用）
  readonly wire: Uint8Array;      // 电路位（原版 Tile.cs sTileHeader）：bit0红 bit1蓝 bit2绿 bit3黄 bit4致动器 bit5已致动(inActive)
  // wire 位掩码常量（对应原版 Tile.cs:396-459 的 wire/wire2/wire3/wire4/actuator/inActive）
  static readonly WIRE_RED = 1;
  static readonly WIRE_BLUE = 2;
  static readonly WIRE_GREEN = 4;
  static readonly WIRE_YELLOW = 8;
  static readonly WIRE_ACTUATOR = 16;
  static readonly WIRE_ACTUATED = 32;
  static readonly WIRE_COLORS = 15; // 四色掩码

  // tile 变更监听（chunk 缓存 / 光照脏标记 / 液体模拟统一从这里拿事件）
  private listeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];

  // ---- 多人联机上报钩子（ClientNet 注入；单机为 null 零开销） ----
  /** 本地 tile 变更上报（协议层 TileOp 收集器）。liquid 通道不报（客户端本地模拟） */
  netReporter: ((op: import('../net/protocol').TileOp) => void) | null = null;
  /** 应用远端操作期间置位：上报钩子静默（防回环） */
  netSuppress = false;

  constructor(w: number, h: number, bufs?: import('../workers/protocol').TileBuffers) {
    this.w = w; this.h = h;
    // buffers 注入（worker transfer 重建路径）：直接以移交的 ArrayBuffer 包装 TypedArray，
    // 跳过分配与拷贝。缺省 explored 由调用方（World.fromPacket）补齐
    if (bufs) {
      this.type = new Uint16Array(bufs.type);
      this.flags = new Uint8Array(bufs.flags);
      this.frameX = new Uint16Array(bufs.frameX);
      this.frameY = new Uint16Array(bufs.frameY);
      this.wall = new Uint16Array(bufs.wall);
      this.liquid = new Uint8Array(bufs.liquid);
      this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(bufs.liquidType);
      this.half = new Uint8Array(bufs.half);
      this.slope = new Uint8Array(bufs.slope);
      this.wire = new Uint8Array(bufs.wire);
      return;
    }
    const n = w * h;
    this.type = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.flags = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.frameX = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.frameY = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.wall = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.liquid = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.half = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.slope = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.wire = new Uint8Array(n);
  }

  idx(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.w + x; }

  inBounds(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < this.w && y < this.h;
  }

  get(x: number, y: number): number { return this.inBounds(x, y) ? this.type[this.idx(x, y)] : 0; }

  isActive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    // 原版 nactive() = active && !inActive（Tile.cs:383-394）：已致动格视为不活跃
    return this.inBounds(x, y) && this.type[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0
      && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) === 0;
  }

  /** 原版 inActive()：致动器翻转后的"缩入地下"态（有 type 但不碰撞不阻挡） */

```

```
    // legacy 无 vid 的原版 4 族（Boss 召唤物/生命水晶，Item.cs SetDefaults useStyle=4）
    return LEGACY_USE_STYLE[def.key] ?? 1;
  }

  /** 使用动画时长（Player.itemAnimationMax = item.useAnimation）：
   *  vi_ 取 vanilla-itemcombat.useAnimation；legacy 工具 def.tool.speed；
   *  无数据 → 30（原版绝大多数消耗品 useAnimation 15-45 的中位近似） */
  private heldUseAnimation(def: (typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number] | null): number {
    if (!def) return 30;
    if (def.tool?.speed) return Math.max(10, def.tool.speed);
    const vid = def.vid ?? viIdFromKey(def.key);
    if (vid >= 0) {
      const ua = itemCombat(vid)?.useAnimation;
      if (ua && ua > 0) return Math.max(4, ua);
    }
    return 30;
  }

  /** 启动一次使用动画（itemAnimation>0 的本项目等价）。
   *  useStyle 5/13（弓/枪/法杖/短剑）附 aim 瞄准角——Renderer 持物姿势与
   *  bodyFrame 行档（Player.cs:35986 useStyle==5 的 itemRotation*direction 档）依赖 */
  private useSwing(def: (typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number] | null, aim?: number): void {
    if (!def) return;
    const us = this.heldUseStyle(def);
    const dur = this.heldUseAnimation(def);
    this.swing = {
      t: dur, dur, item: ITEM_BY_KEY[def.key] ?? 0,
      useStyle: us,
      aim: us === 5 || us === 13 ? aim : undefined,
    };
    this.swingHitSet.clear();
    this.swingTileCutSet.clear();
  }

  /** 双工具分流（Player.cs:45040-45045：tileHammer→锤力 / tileAxe→斧力 / 其余→镐力）。
   *  Hamaxe 族（pick+axe）对树族用斧力、对矿石用镐力；The Axe 族（axe+hammer）
   *  对树族用斧力、其余（锤墙/坡面）走锤。单工具原样返回 */
  private dualToolResolve(
    tool: NonNullable<(typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number]['tool']>,
    targetType: number,
  ): { type: 'pick' | 'axe' | 'hammer'; power: number } {
    const d = tool as NonNullable<(typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number]['tool']> & {
      pickPower?: number; axePower?: number; hammerPower?: number;
    };
    const multi = !!((d.pickPower && d.axePower) || (d.axePower && d.hammerPower) || (d.pickPower && d.hammerPower));
    if (!multi) return { type: tool.type as 'pick' | 'axe' | 'hammer', power: tool.power ?? 10 };

}

  rollExtractinatorDrop, extractinatorMode, extractinatorUseTime,

// ---------- 确定性 RNG（mulberry32；Main.rand.Next 语义） ----------
function seededRand(seed: number): ExtractinatorRand {
  let s = seed >>> 0;
  const next = (n: number) => {
    s = (s + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
    let t = s;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) % Math.max(1, n);
  };
  return { next, nextRange: (a, b) => a + next(b - a) };
}

// ---------- 产物分类（钱币大表/小表产物相同，归并为一类比对） ----------

})();

  st: TileStore, mouse: { x: number; y: number },

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * 一次性诊断脚本的统一 runner(防孤儿进程)。
 *
 * 背景:2026-08-10 发现 7 个 `vite-node tools/_diag-gen.mjs` 孤儿进程,
 * 每个占满 1 核空转 4 小时(脚本 import 链带起不可退出的活句柄,TERM 信号都无法处理)。
 * 约定:以后所有 _diag-*.mjs 一律经本 runner 运行,不再直接 npm exec vite-node。
 *
 * 用法:
 *   node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [传给脚本的参数...]
 *
 * 双层架构:
 *   - 子进程(--_child,内部):导入目标脚本;导入完成 0.5s 后强制 exit(0),
 *     不依赖事件循环自然排空——兜住"脚本主体完成但残留 interval/server 等活句柄"(本次事故形态);
 *   - 父进程(默认):只等子进程退出 + 看门狗;子进程超时(默认 120s)未退 → SIGKILL,exit 2。
 *     父进程事件循环始终空闲,因此子进程无论是同步死循环、永不 resolve 的 await
 *     还是任何未知卡死,都必然在 timeout 内被强杀。
 *
 * 退出码:0 正常 / 1 脚本异常或被信号终止 / 2 超时强杀 / 64 用法错误,其余透传脚本自设退出码。
 */
const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
const FLUSH_MS = 500;

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);

// ---------- 子进程模式:导入目标脚本 ----------
if (argv[0] === '--_child') {
  const target = argv[1];
  try {
    const { pathToFileURL } = await import('node:url');
    const path = await import('node:path');
    await import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.cwd(), target)).href);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[run-diag] 脚本异常:', e);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  // 主体完成:给 stdout 冲刷留 0.5s,无视残留活句柄强制正常退出。
  setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), FLUSH_MS).unref?.();
} else {
  // ---------- 父进程模式:spawn 子进程 + 看门狗 ----------

```

  // ---------- 父进程模式:spawn 子进程 + 看门狗 ----------
  let timeoutMs = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS;
  const rest = [];
  for (const a of argv) {
    if (a.startsWith('--timeout=')) {
      const v = Number(a.slice('--timeout='.length));
      if (Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0) timeoutMs = v;
    } else {
      rest.push(a);
    }
  }
  if (!rest.length) {
    console.error('usage: node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [args...]');
    process.exit(64);
  }

  const { spawn } = await import('node:child_process');
  const path = await import('node:path');
  const target = path.resolve(process.cwd(), rest[0]);

  const { fileURLToPath } = await import('node:url');
  const self = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [self, '--_child', target, ...rest.slice(1)], {
    stdio: 'inherit',
  });

  let done = false;
  const watchdog = setTimeout(() => {
    if (done) return;
    done = true;
    console.error(`[run-diag] 超时 ${timeoutMs}ms,SIGKILL 子进程 (exit 2)`);
    child.kill('SIGKILL');
    process.exit(2);
  }, timeoutMs);

  child.on('exit', (code, signal) => {
    if (done) return;
    done = true;
    clearTimeout(watchdog);
    if (signal) {
      console.error(`[run-diag] 子进程被信号 ${signal} 终止 (exit 1)`);

if (Torches[type]) { if (!Torches[selected.type]) result = i; continue; }
```
所以手持火把时火把不记；继续扫；荧光棒 → result = 荧光棒槽 → break。对，超 200px 时换荧光棒（火把放不了）。好。
   验证：strategy===5 且选中荧光棒槽。
8. 粘滞/还原：host.selected 0（火把），Shift 按住指向泥土 → selected=镐槽；Shift 松开（controlTorch=false）再帧 → selected 还原 0。
9. 无匹配：指向泥土但背包无镐 → 保持原 selected（且策略 3 因 pickRange=-10 不可达 → strategy 0 → case 0 火把/荧光棒！注意：没有镐时 pickRange=-10 → inRange false → strategy 0 → 会选火把。所以"无匹配不切"要用"背包什么都没有"验证 → case 0 无火把荧光棒 → result 保持 selected。
10. 湿格策略 4：目标格液体 + player wet → 水火把/荧光棒优先；且自身不湿时不触发。
11. 换选门：canChangeImmediately=false 时不切。
12. 弹药门：信号枪 930 无荧光弹 → 不选 930；有 931 在弹药槽 54 → 选。

Also a couple of module-level checks:
- SmartSelect 与智能光标独立（不 import SmartCursor 的状态）— implicit.
- wetCollision 单元。

For the env: `screenTopY` and `screenHeight` needed (gravDir=1 → unused but required fields).

Now, mouse position: env.mouseX/mouseY are world px. For "指向格" I set mouse = (tx*16+8, ty*16+8).

Distance >200px check: player center = (px + w/2, py + h/2). Put player at (60*16, 40*16) with w=20,h=42 → center (967, 655). Target empty tile at distance >200px e.g. tile (80, 40) → mouse (1288, 648) → dx=321 → >200 → strategy 5. Must also be within... no range constraint for strategy 0. Good.

But careful: for the >200 test the target tile must be empty (no active tile) so strategy 0. And player wet false so no strategy 4 fallback.

For test 1 (pick): target dirt tile at (62, 41) — within reach (5/3 region): player at tiles 60..61 (x=960..980 → tiles 60,61), region LX=60-5=55, HX=61+5=66, LY=40-3=37, HY=42+3=45 (h=42 → y 640..682 → tiles 40,41,42 → HY=42+3=45). Target (62,41) inside. Good.

For the axe test, tree tile at (63,40)? inside region. Use sheet 5 tile (T.TREE).

Wait — careful with strategy order: tree sheet 5 is in TILE_AXE_SHEETS → strategy 2. But dirt tile sheet 0 → falls to pick branch. Good.

Hammer: altar at (64,40) sheet 26 → strategy 1 — but must be within hammerRange. With hammer in inventory → hammerRange=0 → fine.

Cannon 209 at (65,41): needs frameX/72<3 — setTile default frameX=0 → 0<3 ok. cannonRange requires a torch in inventory.

Extractinator 219 at (66,40): extractItemRange requires an item with ExtractinatorMode>=0 (e.g. silt 424).

Now what about `inRange` with tb=-10 (no such tool): LX = 60-1*(5-10)=... my test inRange: `x = min(20,5)+tb` = 5-10 = -5 → region grows → always true! That's WRONG vs vanilla. Vanilla GetTileRegion with TB: `LX = (int)(position.X/16) - Player.tileRangeX - TB`? Let me check TileReachCheckSettings.GetTileRegion to get exact semantics with negative TB.
````

1	using System;

6	public struct TileReachCheckSettings
7	{
8		public int TileRangeMultiplier;
9	
10		public int? TileReachLimit;
11	
12		public int? OverrideXReach;
13	
14		public int? OverrideYReach;
15	
16		public static readonly TileReachCheckSettings Simple = new TileReachCheckSettings
17		{
18			TileRangeMultiplier = 1,
19			TileReachLimit = 20
20		};
21	
22		public static readonly TileReachCheckSettings Pylons = new TileReachCheckSettings
23		{
24			OverrideXReach = 60,
25			OverrideYReach = 60
26		};
27	
28		public void GetRanges(out int x, out int y)
29		{
30			x = Player.tileRangeX * TileRangeMultiplier;
31			y = Player.tileRangeY * TileRangeMultiplier;
32			if (TileReachLimit.HasValue)
33			{
34				if (x > TileReachLimit.Value)
35				{
36					x = TileReachLimit.Value;
37				}
38				if (y > TileReachLimit.Value)
39				{
40					y = TileReachLimit.Value;
41				}
42			}
43			if (OverrideXReach.HasValue)
44			{
45				x = OverrideXReach.Value;
46			}
47			if (OverrideYReach.HasValue)
48			{
49				y = OverrideYReach.Value;
50			}
51		}
52	
53		public void GetTileRegion(Player player, out int LX, out int LY, out int HX, out int HY, int TB = 0)
54		{
55			GetRanges(out var x, out var y);
56			x += TB;
57			y += TB;
58			LX = (int)(player.position.X / 16f) - x;
59			HX = (int)Math.Ceiling((player.position.X + (float)player.width) / 16f) - 1 + x;
60			LY = (int)(player.position.Y / 16f) - y;
61			HY = (int)Math.Ceiling((player.position.Y + (float)player.height) / 16f) - 1 + y;
62		}
63	
64		public Rectangle GetTileRegion(Player player, int TB = 0)
65		{
66			GetTileRegion(player, out var LX, out var LY, out var HX, out var HY, TB);
67			return new Rectangle(LX, LY, HX - LX, HY - LY);
68		}
69	
70		public void GetWorldRegion(Player player, out int LX, out int LY, out int HX, out int HY, int TB = 0)
71		{
72			GetTileRegion(player, out LX, out LY, out HX, out HY, TB);
73			LX *= 16;
74			LY *= 16;
75			HX *= 16;
76			HY *= 16;
77			HX += 15;
78			HY += 15;
79		}
80	
81		public Rectangle GetWorldRegion(Player player, int TB = 0)
82		{
83			GetWorldRegion(player, out var LX, out var LY, out var HX, out var HY, TB);
84			return new Rectangle(LX, LY, HX - LX, HY - LY);

Wait: x = 5 + (-10) = -5. LX = floor(px/16) - (-5) = tileX + 5. HX = ceil(...)-1 + (-5) = tileX -5. So LX > HX → empty region → Collision.InTileBounds false always. Let me check: LX = tileX+5, HX = tileX-5+... HX = ceil((px+w)/16)-1-5. With px=960,w=20: floor(960/16)=60 → LX=65. ceil(980/16)=62 (980/16=61.25→62) -1-5=56 → HX=56. LX=65 > HX=56 → target must satisfy tx>=65 && tx<=56 → impossible. Good — my earlier worry was wrong; it's still "never in range".

Our Game.inTileRange: `x = min(20,5)+tb` → 5-10 = -5; LX = floor(960/16) - (-5) = 65; HX = ceil(980/16)-1+(-5) = 62-1-5=56. Same → false. And `Math.min(20, 5)` is weird (always 5) but matches vanilla cap logic trivially since tileRangeX=5 < 20. OK — our inTileRange is a faithful equivalent including negative TB.

Now write the test. My test-local inRange replicates the vanilla formula exactly.

{

 "content": "// 智能选取（Smart Select）回归：Player.cs:17283-17562 + SelectedItemState(:383-517) 1:1。\n// 覆盖：Shift 指向镐族/斧族/锤族/提取机（普通+叶绿兑换）/大炮 自动换持、\n// 超 200px 策略 5 修正、湿格策略 4 与湿身兜底、信号枪弹药门、粘滞还原、\n// 无匹配不切、换选门（使用中不切）、目标格换算、wetCollision。\nimport { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';\nimport { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';\nimport { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';\nimport {\n  SmartSelect, EMPTY_SLOT, wetCollision, flareAmmoOf, flareUseAmmoOf,\n  STRATEGY, type SmartSelectEnv, type SmartSelectSlot, type SmartSelectHost,\n} from '../src/player/SmartSelect';\n\n// ---------- 世界/玩家基准 ----------\nconst W = 120, H = 80;\nconst PTX = 60, PTY = 40;                 // 玩家所在格\nconst PX = PTX * 16, PY = PTY * 16;       // position（px）\nconst PW = 20, PH = 42;                   // width / height\nconst ALTAR = TILE_BY_KEY['v_26_altars']!;\nconst EXTRACT = TILE_BY_KEY['v_219_extractinator']!;\nconst CHLORO = TILE_BY_KEY['v_642_chlorophyte_extractinator']!;\nconst CANNON = TILE_BY_KEY['v_209_cannon']!;\nconst MOSS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_184_moss_plants']!;\n\n/** IsInTileInteractionRange 1:1（Player.cs:31548 + TileReachCheckSettings.cs:53-62，\n *  Simple 档 tileRangeX=5/tileRangeY=3 上限 20；TB 可为负 → 区域反转恒 false） */\nfunction inRange(st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number, tb: number): boolean {\n  void st;\n  const x = Math.min(20, 5) + tb, y = Math.min(20, 3) + tb;\n  const LX = Math.floor(PX / 16) - x;\n  const HX = Math.ceil((PX + PW) / 16) - 1 + x;\n  const LY = Math.floor(PY / 16) - y;\n  const HY = Math.ceil((PY + PH) / 16) - 1 + y;\n  return tx >= LX && tx <= HX && ty >= LY && ty <= HY;\n}\n\nconst slot = (vid: number, over: Partial<SmartSelectSlot> = {}): SmartSelectSlot => ({\n  vid, stack: 1, pick: 0, axe: 0, hammer: 0, ammo: 0, useAmmo: 0, tileBoost: 0, ...over,\n});\nconst pick = (vid = 1) => slot(vid, { pick: 40 });\nconst axe = (vid = 10) => slot(vid, { axe: 9 });\nconst hammer = (vid = 2) => slot(vid, { hammer: 35 });\nconst torch = (vid = 8) => slot(vid);\nconst glowstick = (vid = 282) => slot(vid);\n\n/** 58 槽（0-49 背包 + 50-53 钱币 + 54-57 弹药），默认全空 */\nfunction mkSlots(...placed: Array<[number, SmartSelectSlot]>): SmartSelectSlot[] {\n  const s: SmartSelectSlot[] = new Array(58).fill(null).map(() => ({ ...EMPTY_SLOT }));\n  for (const [i, v] of placed) s[i] = v;\n  return s;\n}\n\nfunction mkEnv(\n  st: TileStore,\n  over: Partial<SmartSelectEnv> & { slots?: SmartSelectSlot[]; selectedItem?: number },\n): SmartSelectEnv {\n  return {\n    controlTorch: true,\n    usingGamepad: false,\n    st,\n    mouseX: 0, mouseY: 0,\n    screenTopY: 0, screenHeight: 480,\n    player: { x: PX, y: PY, w: PW, h: PH, wet: false, gravDir: 1 },\n    slots: over.slots ?? mkSlots(),\n    selectedItem: over.selectedItem ?? 0,\n    inRange: (tx, ty, tb) => inRange(st, tx, ty, tb),\n    ...over,\n  } as SmartSelectEnv;\n}\n\n/** 宿主（SelectedItemState 窄视图）：selected 落在本地对象上，便于断言 */\nfunction mkHost(selected = 0, slots: SmartSelectSlot[] = mkSlots(), over: Partial<SmartSelectHost> = {}): SmartSelectHost & { changed: number } {\n  const host = {\n    selected,\n    hotbar: Math.min(selected, 9),\n    canChangeImmediately: true,\n    isSlotEmpty: (i: number) => (slots[i]?.stack ?? 0) <= 0,\n    changed: 0,\n    onSelectionChanged: () => { host.changed++; },\n    ...over,\n  } as SmartSelectHost & { changed: number };\n  return host;\n}\n\nconst mouseAt = (tx: number, ty: number) => ({ mouseX: tx * 16 + 8, mouseY: ty * 16 + 8 });\n\nfunction mkSt(): TileStore { return new TileStore(W, H); }\n\ndescribe('SmartSelect_GetToolStrategy（Player.cs:17472-17528 策略优先级）', () => {\n  it('指向镐族方块 → 策略 3 → 自动换镐', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(62, 41, T.DIRT);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [3, pick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 41) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(3);            // 换到槽 3 的镐\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.PICK);\n  });\n\n  it('指向树（sheet 5，Main.tileAxe）→ 策略 2 → 自动换斧', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(63, 40, T.TREE);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [2, axe()], [3, pick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(63, 40) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(2);\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.AXE);\n  });\n\n  it('指向祭坛（sheet 26，Main.tileHammer）→ 策略 1 → 自动换锤', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(64, 40, ALTAR);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [1, hammer()], [2, axe()], [3, pick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(64, 40) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(1);\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.HAMMER);\n  });\n\n  it('指向提取机 219 → 策略 7 → 换可投入物（泥沙 424）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(66, 40, EXTRACT);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [5, slot(424)]);   // 泥沙：ExtractinatorMode=0\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(66, 40) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(5);\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.EXTRACT);\n  });\n\n  it('指向叶绿提取机 642 → 策略 7 经 ItemTrader 兑换表命中（铜矿 12→锡矿 699）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(66, 40, CHLORO);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [7, slot(12)]);    // 铜矿：CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[12]\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(66, 40) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(7);\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.EXTRACT);\n  });\n\n  it('叶绿提取机：不在兑换表且非可提取物（如泥土块 23? 用普通石头物品 3 亦不在表）→ 不切', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(66, 40, CHLORO);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [7, slot(9999, { stack: 1 })]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(66, 40) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(0);             // 无匹配 → 保持原 selected\n  });\n\n  it('指向大炮 209（frameX/72<3）→ 策略 6 → 换火把点火', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(65, 41, CANNON, 0, 0);           // frameX=0 → 样式 0\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, pick()], [4, torch()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(65, 41) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(4);\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.CANNON);\n  });\n\n  it('大炮样式 ≥3（frameX≥216）不触发策略 6（Player.cs:17490 frameX/72<3 门）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(65, 41, CANNON, 216, 0);         // 216/72=3 → 不算可点火大炮\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, pick()], [4, torch()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(65, 41) });\n    // 非提取/大炮/锤/斧/漆 → 落镐策略 3（大炮格本身可挖）\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.PICK);\n  });\n\n  it('指向长苔藓 184 → 策略 8 → 换刮漆器（1100）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(62, 42, MOSS);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [6, slot(1100)]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 42) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(6);\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.PAINT_SCRAPER);\n  });\n\n  it('策略优先级：锤 > 斧 > 镐（祭坛格不落斧/镐）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(64, 40, ALTAR);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [1, pick()], [2, axe()], [3, hammer()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(64, 40) });\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.HAMMER);\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(3);             // 策略 1 只认 hammer>0 → 槽 3\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('SmartSelectLookup 超 200px 修正（Player.cs:17297-17305）', () => {\n  it('空格远于 200px → 策略 0 修正为 5 → 荧光棒优先于火把', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [1, glowstick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    // 玩家中心 x = 960+10=970；格 (78,40) 中心 x=1256 → dx=286 > 200\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(78, 40) });\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.FAR_LIGHT);\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(1);             // 荧光棒（火把候选被 continue 留给荧光棒）\n  });\n\n  it('空格近于 200px → 策略 0 → 火把优先于荧光棒', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, glowstick()], [1, torch()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(63, 41) });\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.TORCH);\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(1);             // 火把命中即定（后扫不换）\n  });\n\n  it('策略 3/1/2 不受 200px 修正影响', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(64, 41, T.STONE);                // 可达区外一档? 64 在可达区内且距离 ~64px\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [3, pick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(64, 41) });\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.PICK);\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(3);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('SmartSelect_PickToolForStrategy case 4/5 细节（Player.cs:17369-17423）', () => {\n  it('湿格 + 自身湿 → 策略 4 → 水火把优先；荧光棒次之', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.liquid[st.idx(62, 41)] = 128;            // 目标格有液体\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [1, glowstick()], [2, slot(523)]); // 523 水火把\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 41) });\n    env.player.wet = true;\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.WET_LIGHT);\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(2);             // 水火把\n  });\n\n  it('湿格但自身不湿 → 不出策略 4（:17506 要求 wetTile && wet）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.liquid[st.idx(62, 41)] = 128;\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [1, glowstick()], [2, slot(523)]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 41) });\n    env.player.wet = false;\n    expect(ss.strategy).not.toBe(STRATEGY.WET_LIGHT);\n  });\n\n  it('自身湿兜底（:17524-17527）：目标无策略时也出策略 4', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, glowstick(286)]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(63, 41) });\n    env.player.wet = true;\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.WET_LIGHT);\n  });\n\n  it('信号枪 930 无荧光弹 → 弹药门挡下（:17373-17384）；弹药槽 54 有 931 → 放行', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.liquid[st.idx(62, 41)] = 128;\n    // 手持普通火把（槽 0）→ case4 首分支\"手持已是火把\"记候选并 continue；\n    // 信号枪在槽 2；荧光棒缺失\n    const noAmmo = mkSlots([0, torch()], [2, slot(930)]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots: noAmmo, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 41) });\n    env.player.wet = true;\n    expect(flareUseAmmoOf(noAmmo[2])).toBe(931);          // Item.cs:12108 补正\n    expect(flareAmmoOf(slot(931))).toBe(931);             // Item.cs:12121 补正\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(0);                       // 无弹药 → 不切，保持火把\n\n    const withAmmo = mkSlots([0, torch()], [2, slot(930)], [54, slot(931)]);\n    const ss2 = new SmartSelect();\n    const env2 = mkEnv(st, { slots: withAmmo, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 41) });\n    env2.player.wet = true;\n    expect(ss2.lookup(env2)).toBe(2);                     // 有弹药 → 换信号枪\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('SelectedItemState 覆盖/还原（Player.cs:383-517 粘滞语义）', () => {\n  it('Shift 按住换工具、松开还原原槽（overridden :427-429 / 还原 :491-494）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(62, 41, T.DIRT);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [3, pick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 41) });\n    const host = mkHost(0, slots);\n    ss.update(env, host);\n    expect(host.selected).toBe(3);              // 换镐\n    expect(host.changed).toBe(1);\n    // 下一帧仍按住：先还原 0 再覆盖回 3（selected 不变 → 不触发 onChanged）\n    ss.update(env, host);\n    expect(host.selected).toBe(3);\n    expect(host.changed).toBe(1);\n    // 松开 Shift（controlTorch=false → 无覆盖 → 还原原槽）\n    const envOff = { ...env, controlTorch: false };\n    ss.update(envOff, host);\n    expect(host.selected).toBe(0);\n    expect(host.changed).toBe(2);\n    expect(ss.lastStrategy).toBe(-1);           // :17286 松键清粘滞\n  });\n\n  it('换到背包槽(≥10)后物品耗尽 → 回退 hotbar（:499-501）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(62, 41, T.DIRT);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [12, pick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 41) });\n    const host = mkHost(0, slots, { hotbar: 0 });\n    ss.update(env, host);\n    expect(host.selected).toBe(12);             // 可以选背包槽（0-49）\n    // 镐被耗尽（stack 0）→ 下一帧还原 12 时空手 → 回退 hotbar\n    slots[12].stack = 0;\n    ss.update(env, host);\n    expect(host.selected).toBe(12);             // 本帧仍覆盖到 12（覆盖在回退之后 :503）\n    const envOff = { ...env, controlTorch: false };\n    ss.update(envOff, host);\n    expect(host.selected).toBe(0);              // 还原 12 → 空手 → hotbar 0\n  });\n\n  it('使用中（CanChangeSelectedItemImmediately=false）不换不还原（:484 门）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(62, 41, T.DIRT);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [3, pick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 41) });\n    const host = mkHost(0, slots, { canChangeImmediately: false });\n    ss.update(env, host);\n    expect(host.selected).toBe(0);\n    expect(host.changed).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  it('无匹配不切：背包没有任何可换物 → 保持原槽（:17469 返回 selectedItem）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(62, 41, T.DIRT);\n    const slots = mkSlots();                    // 全空（连火把都没有）\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 41) });\n    const host = mkHost(0, slots);\n    ss.update(env, host);\n    expect(host.selected).toBe(0);\n    expect(host.changed).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  it('目标格越界按空 Tile 处理（原版 try/catch 吞越界 :17478/:17526）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [1, glowstick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, mouseX: -40, mouseY: 8 }); // 格 (-3,0)\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(-1);            // controlTorch true 但 lookup 返回保持原槽? 见下\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('SmartSelectLookup_GetTargetTile（SmartSelectGamepadPointer.cs:23-38）', () => {\n  it('鼠标世界坐标 /16 取格', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { selectedItem: 0, mouseX: 62 * 16 + 8, mouseY: 41 * 16 + 8 });\n    ss.lookup(env);\n    // 通过策略反推：空格 → 策略 0（近距）说明目标格是 (62,41) 的空气\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.TORCH);\n    st.setTile(62, 41, T.DIRT);\n    const env2 = mkEnv(st, { selectedItem: 0, mouseX: 62 * 16 + 8, mouseY: 41 * 16 + 8 });\n    ss.lookup(env2);\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.PICK);    // 同一鼠标位置现在指着泥土\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('wetCollision（Collision.cs:1575-1625）', () => {\n  it('命中盒中心盒与液面求交：半格液量浸到下半身即湿', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    // 玩家 y 640..682，中心盒 y 650..671（height/2=21）\n    st.liquid[st.idx(60, 41)] = 200;            // 格 (60,41) y 656..672 液面下移 (256-200)/32*2=3.75\n    expect(wetCollision(st, PX, PY, PW, PH)).toBe(true);\n    st.liquid[st.idx(60, 41)] = 0;\n    expect(wetCollision(st, PX, PY, PW, PH)).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it('液量极低（液面在命中盒之上）不湿', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.liquid[st.idx(60, 41)] = 16;             // 液面下移 (256-16)/32*2=15 → 有效高 1px 且 y=671\n    // 中心盒 y 650..671：survY=656+15=671, 有效高 1 → 671..672 与 650..671 相交（边界）\n    // 原版 AABB 严格不等式：vy+h > surfY（671>671 false）→ 不湿\n    expect(wetCollision(st, PX, PY, PW, PH)).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n"

                // 玩家所在格\nconst PX = PTX * 16, PY = PTY * 16;       // position（px）\nconst PW = 20, PH = 42;                   // width / height\nconst ALTAR = TILE_BY_KEY['v_26_altars']!;\nconst EXTRACT = TILE_BY_KEY['v_219_extractinator']!;\nconst CHLORO = TILE_BY_KEY['v_642_chlorophyte_extractinator']!;\nconst CANNON = TILE_BY_KEY['v_209_cannon']!;\nconst MOSS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_184_moss_plants']!;\n\n/** IsInTileInteractionRange 1:1（Player.cs:31548 + TileReachCheckSettings.cs:53-62，\n *  Simple 档 tileRangeX=5/tileRangeY=3 上限 20；TB=-10 时区域反转恒 false） */\nfunction inRange(st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number, tb: number): boolean {\n  void st;\n  const x = Math.min(20, 5) + tb, y = Math.min(20, 3) + tb;\n  const LX = Math.floor(PX / 16) - x;\n  const HX = Math.ceil((PX + PW) / 16) - 1 + x;\n  const LY = Math.floor(PY / 16) - y;\n  const HY = Math.ceil((PY + PH) / 16) - 1 + y;\n  return tx >= LX && tx <= HX && ty >= LY && ty <= HY;\n}\n\nconst slot = (vid: number, over: Partial<SmartSelectSlot> = {}): SmartSelectSlot => ({\n  vid, stack: 1, pick: 0, axe: 0, hammer: 0, ammo: 0, useAmmo: 0, tileBoost: 0, ...over,\n});\nconst pick = (vid = 1) => slot(vid, { pick: 40 });\nconst axe = (vid = 10) => slot(vid, { axe: 9 });\nconst hammer = (vid = 2) => slot(vid, { hammer: 35 });\nconst torch = (vid = 8) => slot(vid);\nconst glowstick = (vid = 282) => slot(vid);\n\n/** 58 槽（0-49 背包 + 50-53 钱币 + 54-57 弹药），默认全空 */\nfunction mkSlots(...placed: Array<[number, SmartSelectSlot]>): SmartSelectSlot[] {\n  const s: SmartSelectSlot[] = new Array(58).fill(null).map(() => ({ ...EMPTY_SLOT }));\n  for (const [i, v] of placed) s[i] = v;\n  return s;\n}\n\nfunction mkEnv(\n  st: TileStore,\n  over: Partial<SmartSelectEnv> & { slots?: SmartSelectSlot[]; selectedItem?: number },\n): SmartSelectEnv {\n  return {\n    controlTorch: true,\n    usingGamepad: false,\n    st,\n    mouseX: 0, mouseY: 0,\n    screenTopY: 0, screenHeight: 480,\n    player: { x: PX, y: PY, w: PW, h: PH, wet: false, gravDir: 1 },\n    slots: over.slots ?? mkSlots(),\n    selectedItem: over.selectedItem ?? 0,\n    inRange: (tx, ty, tb) => inRange(st, tx, ty, tb),\n    ...over,\n  } as SmartSelectEnv;\n}\n\n/** 宿主（SelectedItemState 窄视图）：selected 落在本地对象上，便于断言 */\nfunction mkHost(selected = 0, slots: SmartSelectSlot[] = mkSlots(), over: Partial<SmartSelectHost> = {}): SmartSelectHost & { changed: number } {\n  const host = {\n    selected,\n    hotbar: Math.min(selected, 9),\n    canChangeImmediately: true,\n    isSlotEmpty: (i: number) => (slots[i]?.stack ?? 0) <= 0,\n    changed: 0,\n    onSelectionChanged: () => { host.changed++; },\n    ...over,\n  } as SmartSelectHost & { changed: number };\n  return host;\n}\n\nconst mouseAt = (tx: number, ty: number) => ({ mouseX: tx * 16 + 8, mouseY: ty * 16 + 8 });\nconst mkSt = () => new TileStore(W, H);\n\ndescribe('SmartSelect_GetToolStrategy（Player.cs:17472-17528 策略优先级）', () => {\n  it('指向镐族方块 → 策略 3 → 自动换镐', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(62, 41, T.DIRT);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [3, pick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 41) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(3);            // 换到槽 3 的镐\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.PICK);\n  });\n\n  it('指向树（sheet 5，Main.tileAxe Main.cs:7157）→ 策略 2 → 自动换斧', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(63, 40, T.TREE);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [2, axe()], [3, pick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(63, 40) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(2);\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.AXE);\n  });\n\n  it('指向祭坛（sheet 26，Main.tileHammer Main.cs:7153）→ 策略 1 → 自动换锤', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(64, 40, ALTAR);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [1, hammer()], [2, axe()], [3, pick()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(64, 40) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(1);\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.HAMMER);\n  });\n\n  it('指向提取机 219 → 策略 7 → 换可投入物（泥沙 424）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(66, 40, EXTRACT);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [5, slot(424)]);             // 无镐? 槽 0 是镐 → 策略 3 选槽 0 自身\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.PICK);    // 落镐策略（大炮格可挖）\n  });\n\n  it('指向长苔藓 184 → 策略 8 → 换刮漆器（1100）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(62, 42, MOSS);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [6, slot(1100)]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(62, 42) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(6);\n    expect(ss.strategy).toBe(STRATEGY.PAINT_SCRAPER);\n  });\n\n  it('策略优先级：提取机 7 > 大炮 6 > 锤 1 > 斧 2 > 刮漆 8 > 镐 3（祭坛格不落斧/镐）', () => {\n    const st = mkSt();\n    st.setTile(64, 40, ALTAR);\n    const slots = mkSlots([0, torch()], [1, pick()], [2, axe()], [3, hammer()]);\n    const ss = new SmartSelect();\n    const env = mkEnv(st, { slots, selectedItem: 0, ...mouseAt(64, 40) });\n    expect(ss.lookup(env)).toBe(3);  "new_string": "/** 由 ItemDef + 三力画像构建槽语义（Game 每帧对 0-57 构建）。\n *  传 into 则原地填充（每帧复用缓冲，防分配热点）；legacy def.tool 画像只覆盖自身类型的\n *  主力（Game 工具判定同序 def.tool 优先），vi_ 双工具（Hamaxe 斧+锤 / Drax 镐+斧）\n *  其余副力保留 itemfunc 全量 */\nexport function smartSelectSlotOf(\n  def: ItemDef | null | undefined,\n  stack: number,\n  itemFunc: { pick?: number; axe?: number; hammer?: number; ammo?: number; useAmmo?: number } | null | undefined,\n  toolOverride?: { type: 'pick' | 'axe' | 'hammer'; power: number } | null,\n  into?: SmartSelectSlot,\n): SmartSelectSlot {\n  const vid = vanillaItemIdOf(def) ?? 0;\n  const pick = itemFunc?.pick ?? 0;\n  const axe = itemFunc?.axe ?? 0;\n  const hammer = itemFunc?.hammer ?? 0;\n  const t = toolOverride;\n  const out: SmartSelectSlot = into ?? {\n    vid: 0, stack: 0, pick: 0, axe: 0, hammer: 0, ammo: 0, useAmmo: 0, tileBoost: 0,\n  };\n  out.vid = vid;\n  out.stack = stack;\n  out.pick = t?.type === 'pick' ? t.power : pick;\n  out.axe = t?.type === 'axe' ? t.power : axe;\n  out.hammer = t?.type === 'hammer' ? t.power : hammer;\n  out.ammo = itemFunc?.ammo ?? 0;\n  out.useAmmo = itemFunc?.useAmmo ?? 0;\n  out.tileBoost = 0;\n  return out;\n}"

113	 *  主力（Game 工具判定同序 def.tool 优先），vi_ 双工具（Hamaxe 斧+锤 / Drax 镐+斧）
114	 *  其余副力保留 itemfunc 全量 */
115	export function smartSelectSlotOf(
116	  def: ItemDef | null | undefined,
117	  stack: number,
118	  itemFunc: { pick?: number; axe?: number; hammer?: number; ammo?: number; useAmmo?: number } | null | undefined,
119	  toolOverride?: { type: 'pick' | 'axe' | 'hammer'; power: number } | null,
120	  into?: SmartSelectSlot,
121	): SmartSelectSlot {
122	  const vid = vanillaItemIdOf(def) ?? 0;
123	  const pick = itemFunc?.pick ?? 0;
124	  const axe = itemFunc?.axe ?? 0;
125	  const hammer = itemFunc?.hammer ?? 0;
126	  const t = toolOverride;
127	  const out: SmartSelectSlot = into ?? {
128	    vid: 0, stack: 0, pick: 0, axe: 0, hammer: 0, ammo: 0, useAmmo: 0, tileBoost: 0,
129	  };
130	  out.vid = vid;
131	  out.stack = stack;
132	  out.pick = t?.type === 'pick' ? t.power : pick;
133	  out.axe = t?.type === 'axe' ? t.power : axe;
134	  out.hammer = t?.type === 'hammer' ? t.power : hammer;
135	  out.ammo = itemFunc?.ammo ?? 0;
136	  out.useAmmo = itemFunc?.useAmmo ?? 0;
137	  out.tileBoost = 0;
138	  return out;
139	}

188	// ===================== 玩家湿身（Collision.WetCollision，Collision.cs:1575-1625） =====================
189	
190	/**
191	 * 玩家是否浸在液体中——Collision.WetCollision 1:1（不含 honey/shimmer 位，Smart Select 只用 wet）。
192	 * 以命中盒中心的 (min(10,width) × height/2) 盒与液体面（液量换算的表面下移）求交。
193	 */
194	export function wetCollision(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number): boolean {
195	  // :1579-1590 盒中心 + num=10 / num2=Height/2（各自截到宽/高）
196	  let num = 10;
197	  let num2 = Math.floor(h / 2);
198	  if (num > w) num = w;
199	  if (num2 > h) num2 = h;
200	  const vx = x + w / 2 - num / 2;
201	  const vy = y + h / 2 - num2 / 2;
202	  // :1591-1594 扫描区外扩 1-2 格
203	  const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(x / 16) - 1);
204	  const x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor((x + w) / 16) + 2);
205	  const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(y / 16) - 1);
206	  const y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor((y + h) / 16) + 2);
207	  for (let i = x0; i <= x1; i++) {
208	    for (let j = y0; j <= y1; j++) {
209	      const idx = st.idx(i, j);
210	      const liquid = st.liquid[idx];
211	      if (liquid <= 0) continue;                       // :1607-1610
212	      // :1612-1617 液面下移：num5=(256-liquid)/32，表面 y += num5*2，有效高 16-=num5*2
213	      const drop = (256 - liquid) / 32;
214	      const surfY = j * 16 + drop * 2;
215	      const surfH = 16 - drop * 2;
216	      // :1618 与中心盒 AABB 求交
217	      if (vx + num > i * 16 && vx < i * 16 + 16 && vy + num2 > surfY && vy < surfY + surfH) {
218	        return true;
219	      }
220	    }
221	  }
222	  return false;
223	}
224

226	
227	export class SmartSelect {
228	  /** _lastSmartCursorToolStrategy（Player.cs:17286 清零 / :17306 写回；手柄粘滞 :17293-17296 用） */
229	  lastStrategy = -1;
230	  /** SelectedItemState.overridden（Player.cs:392）——行为选中前的原槽，松键还原用 */
231	  private overridden = -1;
232	  /** 本帧行为选中的槽（无行为选中 = -1；测试/调试观测用） */
233	  slot = -1;
234	  /** 本帧策略号与湿格（SmartSelectLookup 中间量，观测用） */
235	  strategy = 0;
236	  wetTile = false;
237	
238	  /**
239	   * 每帧驱动 = SelectedItemState.Update（Player.cs:483-517）中与行为选中相关的全部语义：
240	   *   1. 换选门 :484（CanChangeSelectedItemImmediately）
241	   *   2. 还原上帧行为选中 :491-494（无 buffered 且 HasActiveOverride → selected = overridden）
242	   *      + :497 清 overridden（buffered 我们没有——数字键直写 selected，效果等价见 Game 注释）
243	   *   3. :499-501 selected≥10 且空手 → 回退 hotbar
244	   *   4. :503-505 PickItemSelectionOverride（Player.cs:17259-17280）
245	   *      → :17266 SmartSelectLookup → OverrideSelection（:424-434）
246	   *   5. :511-513 selected 变化 → OnSelectionChanged（Player.cs:519-533）
247	   */
248	  update(env: SmartSelectEnv, host: SmartSelectHost): void {
249	    this.slot = -1;
250	    if (!host.canChangeImmediately) return;                    // :484
251	    const prev = host.selected;
252	    if (this.overridden >= 0) host.selected = this.overridden; // :491-494
253	    this.overridden = -1;                                      // :497
254	    if (host.selected >= 10 && host.isSlotEmpty(host.selected)) host.selected = host.hotbar; // :499-501
255	    // PickItemSelectionOverride（Player.cs:17259-17265）：Main.mouseItem 非空 → 选中槽 58。
256	    // 本作鼠标携带物仅存在于背包 UI 拖拽中，而 UI 打开时 controlTorch 已被屏蔽（Input.down
257	    // 的 uiBlocking 门 = TriggersSet.cs:783 键盘 UI 模式让位的等价），故此处无可让位分支。
258	    const slot = this.lookup(env);                             // :17266
259	    this.slot = slot;
260	    if (this.lookupActive(env)) {                              // SmartSelectLookup 返回 true
261	      this.overrideSelection(slot, host);                      // :514-516
262	    }
263	    if (host.selected !== prev) host.onSelectionChanged();     // :511-513
264	  }
265	
266	  /** 行为选中是否激活（SmartSelectLookup 返回值 :17308 = controlTorch） */
267	  private lookupActive(env: SmartSelectEnv): boolean {
268	    return env.controlTorch;
269	  }
270	
271	  /**
272	   * SmartSelectLookup 1:1（Player.cs:17283-17309）。返回行为选中槽。
273	   */
274	  lookup(env: SmartSelectEnv): number {
275	    if (!env.controlTorch) {                                   // :17285-17289
276	      this.lastStrategy = -1;
277	      return -1;
278	    }
279	    const { tX, tY } = this.getTargetTile(env);                // :17291
280	    const got = this.getToolStrategy(tX, tY, env);             // :17292
281	    this.wetTile = got.wetTile;
282	    let strategy = got.toolStrategy;
283	    // :17293-17296 手柄粘滞：UsingGamepad 且上帧有策略 → 沿用。
284	    // 本作 usingGamepad 恒 false（键盘/鼠标输入），分支保留供对照。
285	    if (env.usingGamepad && this.lastStrategy !== -1) strategy = this.lastStrategy;
286	    // :17297-17305 超 200px 修正：策略 0/4（放火把/水下光源）超出放置距离 → 投掷光源策略 5
287	    if (strategy === STRATEGY.TORCH || strategy === STRATEGY.WET_LIGHT) {
288	      const num = Math.abs(env.mouseX - (env.player.x + env.player.w / 2));
289	      const num2 = Math.abs(env.mouseY - (env.player.y + env.player.h / 2)) * 1.3;
290	      if (Math.sqrt(num * num + num2 * num2) > 200) strategy = STRATEGY.FAR_LIGHT;
291	    }
292	    this.lastStrategy = strategy;                              // :17306
293	    this.strategy = strategy;
294	    return this.pickToolForStrategy(tX, tY, strategy, got.wetTile, env); // :17307
295	  }
296	
297	  /**
298	   * SmartSelectLookup_GetTargetTile 1:1（SmartSelectGamepadPointer.cs:23-38）。
299	   * 手柄指针分支（ShouldBeUsed :8-16）本作不达（usingGamepad 恒 false）。
300	   */
301	  private getTargetTile(env: SmartSelectEnv): { tX: number; tY: number } {
302	    const tX = Math.floor(env.mouseX / 16);                     // :25
303	    let tY = Math.floor(env.mouseY / 16);                       // :26
304	    if (env.player.gravDir === -1) {                            // :27-30（反重力未实装，恒不达）
305	      // 原式 tY = (screenPosition.Y + screenHeight − mouseY) / 16（mouseY 为屏幕 px）
306	      const screenMouseY = env.mouseY - env.screenTopY;
307	      tY = Math.floor((env.screenTopY + env.screenHeight - screenMouseY) / 16);
308	    }
309	    return { tX, tY };
310	  }
311	
312	  /**
313	   * SmartSelect_GetAvailableToolRanges 1:1（Player.cs:17530-17562）。
314	   * 每类工具取背包 0-49 中第一件的 tileBoost；没有该类工具 = -10（负 reach → 永不可达，
315	   * 即"没带镐就不会出镐策略"的门）。原版全物品中 tileBoost≠0 的仅 195/196/-1、509/510/+20、
316	   * 1305/+1、3505-3510/-1、5329/5330/+3（Item.cs:3787/:7425/:16116/:31370-31411/:42229），
317	   * 均不属于六类 → 本作槽画像 tileBoost 恒 0，行为与原版一致。
318	   */
319	  private getAvailableToolRanges(env: SmartSelectEnv): {
320	    pickRange: number; axeRange: number; hammerRange: number;
321	    cannonRange: number; extractItemRange: number; paintScraperRange: number;
322	  } {
323	    let pickRange = -10, axeRange = -10, hammerRange = -10;    // :17537-17542
324	    let cannonRange = -10, extractItemRange = -10, paintScraperRange = -10;
325	    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {                             // :17543
326	      const s = env.slots[i] ?? EMPTY_SLOT;
327	      if (s.pick > 0 && pickRange === -10) pickRange = s.tileBoost;        // :17545-17547
328	      if (s.axe > 0 && axeRange === -10) axeRange = s.tileBoost;           // :17548-17550
329	      if (s.hammer > 0 && hammerRange === -10) hammerRange = s.tileBoost;  // :17551-17553
330	      if (TORCH_ITEMS.has(s.vid) && cannonRange === -10) cannonRange = s.tileBoost;            // :17554-17556
331	      if (PAINT_SCRAPER_ITEMS.has(s.vid) && paintScraperRange === -10) paintScraperRange = s.tileBoost; // :17557-17559
332	      if (extractinatorModeOf(s.vid) !== -1 && extractItemRange === -10) extractItemRange = s.tileBoost;  // :17560-17562
333	    }
334	    return { pickRange, axeRange, hammerRange, cannonRange, extractItemRange, paintScraperRange };
335	  }
336	
337	  /**
338	   * SmartSelect_GetToolStrategy 1:1（Player.cs:17472-17528）。
339	   * 原版整段 try/catch 吞越界（:17478/:17526）；本作越界格按空 Tile 处理（TileStore OOB = 0），
340	   * 行为等价且无需异常控制流。
341	   */
342	  private getToolStrategy(tX: number, tY: number, env: SmartSelectEnv): { toolStrategy: number; wetTile: boolean } {
343	    let toolStrategy = 0;                                      // :17475
344	    let flag = false;                                          // :17476
345	    const st = env.st;
346	    const inB = st.inBounds(tX, tY);
347	    // :17480 wetTile：目标格有液体（Tile.liquid > 0；本作 liquid 通道 0-255 同义）
348	    const wetTile = inB ? st.liquid[st.idx(tX, tY)] > 0 : false;
349	    const tileType = inB ? st.get(tX, tY) : 0;
350	    if (tileType !== 0) {                                      // :17481 Main.tile.active()
351	      // 注意：active() = HasTile（含已致动格），不是 nactive()——TileStore.isActive 不可用
352	      const type = sheetOf(tileType);
353	      const r = this.getAvailableToolRanges(env);              // :17479
354	      if ((type === 219 || type === 642) && env.inRange(tX, tY, r.extractItemRange)) {
355	        toolStrategy = STRATEGY.EXTRACT;                       // :17486-17489
356	        flag = true;
357	      } else if (type === 209 && Math.floor(cannonFrameX(st, tX, tY) / 72) < 3
358	        && env.inRange(tX, tY, r.cannonRange)) {
359	        toolStrategy = STRATEGY.CANNON;                        // :17490-17492（frameX/72 整除 = 大炮样式 <3）
360	        flag = true;
361	      } else if (TILE_HAMMER_SHEETS.has(type) && env.inRange(tX, tY, r.hammerRange)) {
362	        toolStrategy = STRATEGY.HAMMER;                        // :17493-17495
363	        flag = true;
364	      } else if (TILE_AXE_SHEETS.has(type) && env.inRange(tX, tY, r.axeRange)) {
365	        toolStrategy = STRATEGY.AXE;                           // :17496-17498
366	        flag = true;
367	      } else if (type === 184 && env.inRange(tX, tY, r.paintScraperRange)) {
368	        toolStrategy = STRATEGY.PAINT_SCRAPER;                 // :17499-17501（长苔藓 → 刮漆）
369	        flag = true;
370	      } else if (env.inRange(tX, tY, r.pickRange)) {
371	        toolStrategy = STRATEGY.PICK;                          // :17502-17504
372	        flag = true;
373	      }
374	    } else if (wetTile && env.player.wet) {
375	      toolStrategy = STRATEGY.WET_LIGHT;                       // :17506-17508（湿格且自身湿 → 水下光源）
376	      flag = true;
377	    }
378	    if (!flag && env.player.wet) toolStrategy = STRATEGY.WET_LIGHT; // :17524-17527（兜底：湿身即水下光源）
379	    return { toolStrategy, wetTile };
380	  }
381	
382	  /**
383	   * SmartSelect_PickToolForStrategy 1:1（Player.cs:17321-17470）。
384	   * 在 inventory 0-49 找策略对应工具；无匹配返回 selectedItem（即"不切"）。
385	   */
386	  private pickToolForStrategy(
387	    tX: number, tY: number, toolStrategy: number, wetTile: boolean, env: SmartSelectEnv,
388	  ): number {
389	    let result = env.selectedItem;                             // :17323
390	    const selectedVid = env.slots[env.selectedItem]?.vid ?? 0; // inventory[selectedItem].type
391	    slotLoop: for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {                   // :17324
392	      const s = env.slots[i] ?? EMPTY_SLOT;
393	      const type = s.vid;                                      // :17326 inventory[i].type
394	      switch (toolStrategy) {
395	        case STRATEGY.TORCH: {                                 // :17339-17349
396	          if (TORCH_ITEMS.has(type)) { result = i; break; }    // 火把命中即定（后扫不换）
397	          if (GLOWSTICK_ITEMS.has(type)) result = i;           // 荧光棒先记候选
398	          continue;                                            // 继续找火把（火把优先级更高）
399	        }
400	        case STRATEGY.HAMMER: {                                // :17351-17355
401	          if (s.hammer <= 0) continue;
402	          result = i;
403	          break;
404	        }
405	        case STRATEGY.AXE: {                                   // :17357-17361
406	          if (s.axe <= 0) continue;
407	          result = i;
408	          break;
409	        }
410	        case STRATEGY.PICK: {                                  // :17363-17367
411	          if (s.pick <= 0) continue;
412	          result = i;
413	          break;
414	        }
415	        case STRATEGY.WET_LIGHT: {                             // :17369-17395
416	          if (!GLOWSTICK_ITEMS.has(type) && type !== FLARE_GUN
417	            && TORCH_ITEMS.has(type) && !WATER_TORCH_ITEMS.has(type)) {
418	            // 普通火把：手持已是火把则不动，否则记候选继续扫（给后面的荧光棒/水火把留机会）
419	            if (!TORCH_ITEMS.has(selectedVid)) result = i;
420	            continue;
421	          }
422	          if (GLOWSTICK_ITEMS.has(type) && wetTile) {
423	            result = i;
424	          } else if (type === FLARE_GUN && wetTile) {
425	            if (!this.hasAmmoFor(env, i)) continue;            // :17373-17384 弹药门（扫 57→0）
426	            result = i;
427	          } else {
428	            if (!WATER_TORCH_ITEMS.has(type)) continue;
429	            result = i;
430	          }
431	          break;
432	        }
433	        case STRATEGY.FAR_LIGHT: {                             // :17397-17423
434	          if (TORCH_ITEMS.has(type)) {
435	            // 手持已是火把则不动（放不到也保持火把），否则记候选继续扫
436	            if (!TORCH_ITEMS.has(selectedVid)) result = i;
437	            continue;
438	          }
439	          if (type === FLARE_GUN) {
440	            if (!this.hasAmmoFor(env, i)) continue;            // :17408-17420 弹药门
441	            result = i;
442	          } else {
443	            if (!GLOWSTICK_ITEMS.has(type)) continue;
444	            result = i;
445	          }
446	          break;
447	        }
448	        case STRATEGY.CANNON: {                                // :17425-17429
449	          if (!TORCH_ITEMS.has(type)) continue;                // 大炮点火 = 火把（PlaceThing_CannonBall :41139）
450	          result = i;
451	          break;
452	        }
453	        case STRATEGY.EXTRACT: {                               // :17431-17446
454	          // TryGettingItemTraderFromBlock（Player.cs:41043-41052）：仅 tile 642 叶绿提取机
455	          if (isChlorophyteTraderBlock(env, tX, tY) && chlorophyteTradeAccepts(s)) {
456	            result = i;
457	            break;
458	          }
459	          if (extractinatorModeOf(type) < 0) continue;         // :17441-17444
460	          result = i;
461	          break;
462	        }
463	        case STRATEGY.PAINT_SCRAPER: {                         // :17448-17452
464	          if (!PAINT_SCRAPER_ITEMS.has(type)) continue;
465	          result = i;
466	          break;
467	        }
468	        default:
469	          continue;                                            // :17454-17455
470	      }
471	      break slotLoop;                                          // :17457（C# switch 后的 break = 跳出 for）
472	    }
473	    return result;                                             // :17469
474	  }
475	
476	  /**
477	   * 信号枪弹药门 1:1（Player.cs:17374-17384 / :17410-17420）：
478	   * inventory[57..0] 中任一物品 ammo == 该槽 useAmmo 且 stack>0。
479	   * 荧光弹族的 useAmmo/ammo 提取缺口按原版源码补正（见 FLARE_GUN_USE_AMMO 注释）。
480	   */
481	  private hasAmmoFor(env: SmartSelectEnv, slotIndex: number): boolean {
482	    const useAmmo = flareUseAmmoOf(env.slots[slotIndex] ?? EMPTY_SLOT);
483	    for (let n = 57; n >= 0; n--) {
484	      const s = env.slots[n] ?? EMPTY_SLOT;
485	      if (flareAmmoOf(s) === useAmmo && s.stack > 0) return true;
486	    }
487	    return false;
488	  }
489	
490	  /** OverrideSelection 1:1（Player.cs:424-434） */
491	  private overrideSelection(item: number, host: SmartSelectHost): void {
492	    if (!host.canChangeImmediately || item === host.selected) return;   // :425-426
493	    if (this.overridden === -1) this.overridden = host.selected;        // :427-429 记住原槽
494	    if (item === this.overridden) this.overridden = -1;                 // :430-432 切回原槽 = 覆盖结束
495	    host.selected = item;                                               // :433
496	  }
497	}
498	
499	// ===================== 槽语义辅助（原版数据补正） =====================
500	
501	/** Item.useAmmo（信号枪族按 Item.cs:12108 补正） */
502	export function flareUseAmmoOf(s: SmartSelectSlot): number {
503	  return FLARE_GUN_USE_AMMO[s.vid] ?? s.useAmmo;
504	}
505	
506	/** Item.ammo（荧光弹族按 Item.cs:12121/:19106 补正） */
507	export function flareAmmoOf(s: SmartSelectSlot): number {
508	  return FLARE_AMMO_ITEMS.has(s.vid) ? AMMO_FLARE : s.ammo;
509	}
510	
511	/** ItemID.Sets.ExtractinatorMode[type]（ItemID.cs:1104；EXTRACTINATOR_MODE = 全表直录） */
512	function extractinatorModeOf(vid: number): number {
513	  return EXTRACTINATOR_MODE[vid] ?? -1;
514	}
515	
516	/** TryGettingItemTraderFromBlock（Player.cs:41043-41052）：tile 642 → ChlorophyteExtractinator */
517	function isChlorophyteTraderBlock(env: SmartSelectEnv, tX: number, tY: number): boolean {
518	  const st = env.st;
519	  if (!st.inBounds(tX, tY)) return false;
520	  return sheetOf(st.get(tX, tY)) === 642;
521	}
522	
523	/**
524	 * ItemTrader.TryGetTradeOption 1:1（ItemTrader.cs:66-80）：
525	 * 逐条 TradeOption 比较 TakingItemType == item.type && item.stack >= TakingItemStack。
526	 * ChlorophyteExtractinator 的 33 条选项全部 TakingItemStack=1（ItemTrader.cs:82-108，
527	 * Interchangable/CyclicLoop/FromAny 都经 AddOption_OneWay(taking, 1, giving, 1)），
528	 * 因此等价于"CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES 命中且 stack≥1"。
529	 */
530	function chlorophyteTradeAccepts(s: SmartSelectSlot): boolean {
531	  return s.stack >= 1 && CHLOROPHYTE_TRADES[s.vid] !== undefined;
532	}
533	
534	/** Tile.frameX（px；TileStore.frameX 通道与原版同单位——WldImport 直拷 wld 帧） */
535	function cannonFrameX(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): number {
536	  return st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.frameX[st.idx(x, y)] : 0;
537	}
538

process.exitCode = 1; }\n  else {\n    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500));\n    const prep = await page.evaluate(() => {\n      const g = window.__swGame;\n      const ITEMS = window.__swItems;\n      const inv = g.player.inv;\n      inv.slots[0] = { id: ITEMS['torch'], stack: 99 };\n      inv.slots[3] = { id: ITEMS['copper_pickaxe'], stack: 1 };\n      inv.selected = 0;\n      // 玩家正下方找第一格泥土\n      const st = g.world.store;\n      const dirt = g.tileByKey['dirt'];\n      const ptx = Math.floor(g.player.cx / 16);\n      const pty = Math.floor((g.player.y + g.player.h) / 16);\n      let target = null;\n      for (let y = pty; y < pty + 12 && !target; y++) {\n        if (st.type[st.idx(ptx, y)] === dirt) target = { x: ptx, y };\n      }\n      if (!target) return { ok: false };\n      // 鼠标指到该格中心（屏幕 px）：世界 → 屏幕（camera 为视区中心）\n      const zoom = g.camera.zoom;\n      const canvas = g.renderer.canvas;\n      g.input.mouseX = ((target.x * 16 + 8) - g.camera.x) * zoom + canvas.width / 2;\n      g.input.mouseY = ((target.y * 16 + 8) - g.camera.y) * zoom + canvas.height / 2;\n      return { ok: true, target, mouseX: g.input.mouseX, mouseY: g.input.mouseY };\n    });\n    if (!prep.ok) { console.error('FAIL 未找到玩家下方泥土'); process.exitCode = 1; }\n    else {\n      await page.keyboard.down('Shift');\n      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600));\n      const held = await page.evaluate(() => ({\n        selected: window.__swGame.player.inv.selected,\n        strategy: window.__swGame.smartSelect.strategy,\n        slot: window.__swGame.smartSelect.slot,\n      }));\n      await page.keyboard.up('Shift');\n      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600));\n      const released = await page.evaluate(() => ({ selected: window.__swGame.player.inv.selected }));\n      console.log('target', JSON.stringify(prep.target), 'mouse', prep.mouseX, prep.mouseY);\n      console.log('Shift 按住:', JSON.stringify(held));\n      console.log('Shift 松开:', JSON.stringify(released));\n      const pass = held.strategy === 3 && held.selected === 3 && released.selected === 0;\n      console.log(pass ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL');\n      if (!pass || errors.length) { console.error('errors:', errors.slice(0, 5)); process.exitCode = pass ? 0 : 1; }\n    }\n  }\n} finally {\n  await browser.close().catch(() => {});\n}\n"
}
